  ┃┃┃ ★ = also in the STRONG 20    * = also in the RECOMMENDED list            ┃┃┃
  ┃┃┃ + = digits reversed of a table number whose series is there              ┃┃┃
  ┃┃┃ A wide list: it holds the result often by size alone, NOT a proven edge. ┃┃┃
